Numerical cases

All authored coefficients and bounds are saved in scenarios.json. The returned-result column identifies the first trial; every trial, including failures, appears below and in CSV. Original-space primal, stationarity, sign and complementarity checks are numerical. Certificates, justified bounds, basis and complete minimizer families are unavailable. Original norm costs and squared solver costs remain distinct.

Inclusive latency (ns)

Successful input-to-verified-result latency excludes object destruction, serialization, process startup and application orchestration. Exceptional intervals labelled end_to_end_including_unwind include owned cleanup and remain excluded from successful summaries. Nested phases are not added to end-to-end. Range means minimum to maximum; it is not a confidence interval. Fewer than five or failed/incomplete trials have no successful latency summary.

Exact phase boundaries
input_publication
Copy authored input and validate/deep-copy the immutable program.
registration_preparation
Register the actual backend, select and snapshot the prepared pipeline.
workspace_creation
Create the exclusive bound workspace.
public_run
Run the public operation and consume status/witness bits/residual presence/diagnostic length. Includes native translation, solve, logging and in-run checks.
independent_verification
Check literal answers, original constraints, identities and numerical evidence after the public call.
end_to_end
Input-to-verified-result interval including the preceding phases and intervening harness work, before destruction.
record_serialization
Build trial JSON after numerical timing; separate following event excludes write/flush.

Native phase observations

Configured budgets are not execution evidence or elapsed time. Returned status pairs establish an observed call, not mathematical assurance. Native phase elapsed time is unavailable: not_exposed_by_adapter.
